# Beat Vegas Post-mortem: rated games vs outcomes

Generated by `scripts/post_mortem.py`. Pushes are excluded from the hit rate and count as staked. Every percentage carries its n and a 95% Wilson interval; P(>52.4%) is the factor ledger's Beta posterior against the -110 breakeven. Separating a 55% bettor from breakeven takes about 2,300 bets, so these tables rule out large edges and find large mistakes, nothing finer.

## Scope `hist_2023_25` (computed 2026-10-07T14:24:17Z, 3596 graded games)

- Ratings are the stored walk-forward gbm_v1 rows (2023-25); every line is a proxy, not a real 1H market.
- 'flat' grades at the 0.52 x full-game line the ratings were built on; 'step' grades at the fair spread-aware line (0.4975 / 0.5375 at spread 21+). The step column is the honest one.
- No Hard Rock prices existed historically, so the price, off-market and QB gates cannot be applied; 'followed the system' is the gap gate plus the weekly cap of 5.
- About 2,300 bets separate a 55% bettor from breakeven at 80% power; this data cannot confirm a realistic edge.
- 'real' grades at the us-region consensus first-half close (no Hard Rock: it did not exist historically), captured once about 30 minutes before kickoff from The Odds API history; 1897 of 3596 rated games have one. Only those games appear in the real column.
- 'fg' grades the SAME picks (selected on the real 1H gap) as FULL-GAME unders at the captured pre-kick full-game consensus close; 1897 of 3596 rated games have one.

### What to change

- **OK · gap_gate** — Gap discriminates (55.6% at 1.75–3, 55.6% at 3+, vs 47.6% below 0); keep the 1.75 gap bar as a ranking rule. Followed-the-system hit rate against the estimated line is 55.9% (n 166), at or above breakeven.
- **OK · gap_dip** — 1–1.75 band hits 49.6% vs 55.6% at 1.75–3 (difference +6.0 pts, about 1.0 SE). Within noise: 1.75 is a ranking convenience, not where an edge starts.
- **OK · strong_label** — Gap 3+ hits 55.6% vs 55.6% for 1.75–3: no extra hit rate; keep 3.0 as a sort label only.
- **OK · division** — Non-FBS games hit 48.8% vs 48.8% FBS-vs-FBS (-0.0 pts). Little difference under the fair proxy.
- **WATCH · early_season** — Weeks 3–6 hit 46.5% vs 51.1% in weeks 7–10. Early-season gaps look weaker; consider discounting them or raising the games-played minimum.
- **WATCH · contrast_full_game_total** — Wins and losses differ on full-game total: wins average 57.52, losses 55.05 (d=+0.37, q=0.06, n 101/64). A hypothesis to test on real 2026 lines, not a rule.
- **WATCH · contrast_line** — Wins and losses differ on first-half line: wins average 57.52, losses 55.18 (d=+0.35, q=0.06, n 101/64). A hypothesis to test on real 2026 lines, not a rule.
- **WATCH · contrast_fh_pf_sum** — Wins and losses differ on combined first-half points scored: wins average 34.04, losses 30.36 (d=+0.43, q=0.06, n 101/64). A hypothesis to test on real 2026 lines, not a rule.
- **WATCH · contrast_full_game_total** — Wins and losses differ on full-game total: wins average 56.42, losses 54.71 (d=+0.25, q=0.05, n 156/133). A hypothesis to test on real 2026 lines, not a rule.
- **WATCH · contrast_line** — Wins and losses differ on first-half line: wins average 56.38, losses 54.65 (d=+0.26, q=0.05, n 156/133). A hypothesis to test on real 2026 lines, not a rule.
- **WATCH · contrast_gap** — Wins and losses differ on gap: wins average 3.31, losses 2.93 (d=+0.32, q=0.05, n 156/133). A hypothesis to test on real 2026 lines, not a rule.
- **WATCH · contrast_off_ppa** — Wins and losses differ on combined offensive efficiency: wins average 0.40, losses 0.36 (d=+0.28, q=0.05, n 156/133). A hypothesis to test on real 2026 lines, not a rule.
- **WATCH · contrast_fh_pf_sum** — Wins and losses differ on combined first-half points scored: wins average 33.84, losses 30.59 (d=+0.39, q=0.04, n 156/133). A hypothesis to test on real 2026 lines, not a rule.
- **WATCH · contrast_kick_hour_et** — Wins and losses differ on kickoff hour (ET): wins average 17.53, losses 16.43 (d=+0.31, q=0.05, n 156/133). A hypothesis to test on real 2026 lines, not a rule.
- **OK · multiple_comparisons** — 336 comparisons were run, so about 17 would look meaningful by luck alone. Only differences that clear that bar by a wide margin are flagged.

### Does the gap survive spread and total controls?

- flat/fbs_only: standardized logistic coefficients (1929 games): gap_flat +0.13, spread_abs -0.05, full_game_total +0.11, wx_wind +0.02
- step/fbs_only: standardized logistic coefficients (1884 games): gap_step +0.08, spread_abs -0.02, full_game_total +0.11, wx_wind +0.03
- real/fbs_only: standardized logistic coefficients (1868 games): gap_real +0.08, spread_abs +0.01, full_game_total +0.13, wx_wind +0.03
- fg/fbs_only: standardized logistic coefficients (1871 games): gap_fg +0.02, spread_abs +0.01, full_game_total +0.14, wx_wind +0.01
- flat/all: standardized logistic coefficients (3596 games): gap_flat +0.05, spread_abs -0.15, full_game_total +0.04, wx_wind +0.03
- step/all: standardized logistic coefficients (3514 games): gap_step +0.04, spread_abs -0.10, full_game_total +0.04, wx_wind +0.04
- real/all: standardized logistic coefficients (1868 games): gap_real +0.08, spread_abs +0.01, full_game_total +0.13, wx_wind +0.03
- fg/all: standardized logistic coefficients (1871 games): gap_fg +0.02, spread_abs +0.01, full_game_total +0.14, wx_wind +0.01

### Overfitting control

- Deflated Sharpe of `cap5/step/fbs_only` against 32 tried configurations: 0.18 (≥ 0.95 would be significant).

## Scope `live_2026` (computed 2026-10-07T14:24:17Z, 412 graded games)

- 'hr' grades the under at Hard Rock's own number and price when the card was built, 'hr_close' at Hard Rock's own pre-kick close, 'market' at the consensus number at build time, 'market_close' at the consensus close.
- 'Qualifying' is the paper ledger: every game whose Hard Rock 1H line sat >= 1.75 above ours, tagged by the gate that blocked a real bet (the 'blocker' dimension).
- Counts, not rates, until a bucket has 30 graded games.
